    - **Keys and the phone.** <kbd>F9</kbd> skips, <kbd>F10</kbd> holds or resumes the talk, <kbd>F12</kbd> calls up the next race, shown or not, and from the **Broadcast** window too. The phone remote has **SKIP**, **HOLD TALK** and **CALL UP** under **BETWEEN RACES**.
    - **The Broadcast window** has a **Between races** line saying what's next and when ("Call-up for Heat 3 in 40s"), with **Skip Next Call-up** beside it.
    - **The phone and the PA agree.** The call-up is always for the race the drivers' phones show as "You're up next". Every line in the gap goes out with captions and on Listen Along, and the console says once why a call-up was held ("inside the quiet before the call").
    - **The On Air bar** says "Between-race talk held - press Resume Talk or F10" while talk is held, and names what **Skip** would skip ("Skip: Race Recap"). Let go of the hold and talk picks up after the space between lines.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC; nothing changes on the relay.
- **The bottom bar stays put on an iPhone (31 October).** On an iPhone, Safari and Chrome hide their own toolbars as the page scrolls and bring them back on the first tap near the bottom of the screen, so the club pages' bottom bar jumped up and that tap was lost. On a phone the pages now scroll inside themselves, so the browser keeps its toolbars where they are and the bar doesn't move. The browser's toolbars no longer tuck away as you scroll, and pulling down doesn't reload the page (the pages refresh themselves). Laptops and tablets are unchanged. Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **Lap window tiles show their whole labels (31 October).** The tops of LAPS, BEST, AVERAGE and RUN in a lap window were cut off, and its OFF BEST column title read "OFF BES". Both now show in full at any text size. A lap window dragged as tall as it goes now stops at the last whole lap that fits on the screen. Update HeatCaller on the race PC; nothing changes on the relay.
- **Lap times said as the screen shows them, and no stale running order (31 October).** Two fixes for calls that didn't match the screen. See [Every announcement](announcer/voice.md#every-announcement).
    - **Every thousandth.** The voice used to round lap times to hundredths, so a 6.935 on the screen was called "six ninety-four". It now says the whole time in the same race-caller rhythm: "six nine thirty-five", "five oh oh six". A time with nothing in the thousandths reads as before ("five oh six", "five flat"). This covers every lap call, fast lap, record and lap time in the commentary.
    - **The running order waits at most 3 seconds.** The position report waits its turn behind a line that's already being spoken. It used to wait as long as it took, then read the order as it was when the report was written, which on short laps could be an order the screen had already changed. Now a report that waited more than 3 seconds is dropped, and the console says so. The next report comes round as usual.
    - **Lap times in my ear sound the same (31 October).** The relay's spoken laps on the **Live** page and a driver's own night now say the whole time too: "six nine thirty-five" in the Daniel voice, played as two short clips back to back, and "6.935" in the phone's own voice. A time with nothing in the thousandths sounds as before. Run `lap-voice-render.cmd` in the relay folder once to make the 260 new clips (about 2,700 characters, roughly 11 cents on your ElevenLabs key; the clips you have are kept), then redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). Until the new clips are made, a time with a thousandth is said by the phone's voice.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC and redeploy the relay.
- **HeatCaller on the club's pages (30 October).** The relay pages say **on HeatCaller** under the club's name and end with a small sun and HEATCALLER mark; tap it for a short card about HeatCaller, with **Bring HeatCaller to your track**. **Live** shows a **LIVE** badge while a race is staging, counting down or running. Shared result cards and the night's share picture carry the sun, and the first-night guide ends with "Timing and race calls by HeatCaller." The home-screen icon is now HeatCaller's sun for every club, logo or not, and the relay's own address, "No club here" and a paused club are drawn in HeatCaller's look. Your club's name, logo and colors stay in charge, and the mark is always on. See [Looks and sharing links](online/index.md#looks-and-sharing-links). Redeploy the relay.
- **HeatCaller's mark on everything the app makes (31 October).** Everything HeatCaller makes for people to see now signs off with the small sun and the HEATCALLER wordmark, like the club's relay pages. The club's own name, logo and colors stay in charge. The mark is always on.
    - **Highlight videos.** The end card now says "Timed and called by" over the full-color sun and HEATCALLER, in place of the small grey "Powered by HeatCaller".
    - **Big Screen.** A small sun and HEATCALLER sit in the bottom right of the countdown band, in the club's colors, like a TV channel's corner logo. The countdown stays in the middle of the screen.
    - **Stream graphics.** The race tower on the OBS page has a HEATCALLER credit along its foot.
    - **Phone pages on the club Wi-Fi.** Live Results, Check In, Race Remote, Manage, Coach, Message the announcer, Who wins? and Finish Camera end with the same HEATCALLER mark as the relay pages, linking to heatcaller.com.
    - **Printed sheets.** Heat sheets, run sheets, results sheets, report cards and QR badge sheets share one footer: a black sun, HEATCALLER and heatcaller.com. The track plan and the join poster carry the mark too.
    - **Results picture.** The picture made for group chats says "Timed and called by HeatCaller" beside the full-color sun, the same as the relay's cards.
    - **Emails to drivers.** Night, PIN and sign-up invite emails end with one line: "Timing by HeatCaller - heatcaller.com".
    - **Event report.** Signed with the mark and a link to heatcaller.com.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC; nothing changes on the relay.
- **Several lap breakdowns open at once (30 October).** On Race Control, each driver's lap breakdown is now a small window of its own instead of a popup that closed when you clicked away. Open one for as many drivers as you like, drag each by the driver's name (onto a second monitor too), and drag the bottom edge to show from 4 to 40 laps. Close one with its **X**, **Esc**, or by double-clicking the driver again. Each window goes back where the last one in its place was left, and never opens on the Big Screen's monitor. Right-click a driver for **Line up lap windows** (neat columns, in running order) and **Close all lap windows**. They all close when the next race is called, so each heat starts on a clean screen. The flag keys keep working while one is in front. See [Running a race](race-control/running-a-race.md). Update HeatCaller on the race PC; nothing changes on the relay.
- **Home earns its keep (30 October).** The club's home page now changes with the night. See [Getting around](online/index.md#getting-around).
    - **Between nights** it says "No racing right now" and when the club races, then recaps the last race night: each final's winner, the fastest lap, the points leader and that night's new achievements, with a link to all of that night's results. While racing it shows what's on track; before a night, the club's preview. The old **Up next** and **Latest results** lists are gone, since Schedule and Results have them.
    - **You.** On a phone that remembers its driver, a strip shows their best finish last time, their standings place, their newest achievement and **Open your night**.
    - **New here?** A card for visitors with where and when the club races and how to join, typed into the **Look** card on HeatCaller's **Club Website** page (**Where we race**, **When we race**, **How to join**). Leave them empty for no card.
    - **Our tracks** moved from Results to the bottom of Home, today's layout first, each opening the Tracks page.
    - **Button tabs.** Results' tabs (**Sessions**, **Standings**, **Achievements**) are bigger and look like buttons, the page you're on filled in. Me's race tabs (**Lap stats**, **Lap times**, **Lap chart**) and jump bar match.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC and redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). With an older HeatCaller there's no **New here?** card.
- **Club pages clear of the phone's clock (30 October).** On some phones, and always from a home-screen icon, the club's pages drew under the status bar, so each page looked scrolled down a little, the title sat behind the clock and scrolling up only bounced. The pages now start below the status bar, and the bars that stay at the top while you scroll (My Night's jump bar, the Manager's tabs) stop below it too. Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **Standings, Tracks and Achievements under Results (30 October).** On the club's pages, **Results** now has a row of tabs at the top: **Sessions**, **Standings**, **Tracks** and **Achievements**, with **Results** lit on all four (Tracks moved to Home the same day). The **Explore** tiles at the bottom of Home are gone, since most repeated the bar; while online check-in is open, Home has a **Check in: tap Me and type your PIN** line instead. On a laptop the top row is now the same five tabs as the phone bar. Page addresses don't change. See [Getting around](online/index.md#getting-around). Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **The poster and emails mention Me (29 October).** The club poster's line now reads "Live timing, schedule and results - and your own races under Me." A driver's night email says that opening their night once on their phone lets **Me** bring them back to it, and gives the club's own page for live timing, the running order and results. The PIN email says the same PIN opens their own races under **Me** on race day. Update HeatCaller on the race PC; nothing changes on the relay.
- **The help brought up to date on commentary and videos (29 October).** See [Commentary](announcer/commentary.md).
    - **The built-in model, not the old separate program.** The [Commentary](announcer/commentary.md) page now explains the model built into HeatCaller: what it is, that it needs nothing installed, that it skips a PC with under 2 GB of memory, and how it times itself and switches off on a PC too slow for it. Its troubleshooting covers the built-in model's own console lines. **What HeatCaller does**, the **FAQ** and the **Glossary** say the same.
    - **Console messages.** When the relay can't write commentary, the console and the **Test Connection** result now say the model built into HeatCaller writes instead instead.
    - **Podium videos.** The **Recording** card on the Commentary page, the **Glossary** and the privacy notice draft no longer say the podium's videos go on the club's page. Every highlight video stays on the race PC.
    - Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`) to pick up its new wording when commentary is switched off there. Nothing else changes on the relay.
- **A PIN on Me checks you in (30 October).** On the club's **Me** tab, a driver who types their right personal PIN and taps **Check in** is checked in for tonight and their night opens, in one go. **Me** no longer links to the separate check-in page. A driver who's already checked in is left as they are. See [Opening your night with your PIN](online/index.md#opening-your-night-with-your-pin). Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **My night on race night (29 October).** The rest of the night page's **Right now** card is laid out as planned. See [Right now](online/index.md#right-now).
    - **Race-day jobs in one row.** **Check in**, **Your transponder**, **Lap times in my ear** and **Your battery pack** sit side by side on a wide screen and one under the other on a phone. On race night the battery pack is here instead of in **Your details**, and a pack they've picked stays picked when the card refreshes.
    - **Marshal duty on one line**: "Your marshal duty: Q2 Heat 2 - Turn 3 · 1 race before it."
    - **Right now on the jump bar.** While the card is there, the jump bar's first button goes back up to it.
    - **Bigger buttons** on the jump bar, the stat picker and the race tabs, for thumbs.
    - Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). Nothing changes on the race PC.
- **The race board first on Live (29 October).** On the club's **Live** page the leaderboard now sits straight under the race's flag bar, at the top, so a phone shows it without scrolling. **Listen to the announcer**, **Captions** and **Lap times in my ear** follow it. Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **Lap times in my ear, in the HeatCaller lap voice (29 October).** On the relay's **Live** page and a driver's own night, laps are now read out in one ElevenLabs voice, **Daniel**, for every club, whatever announcer a club picked in its Cast tab: "Lap seven, nine forty-two, best lap, P two, four tenths back". The phone plays short clips recorded once, so a race night costs nothing however many drivers listen, and nothing about any driver goes to ElevenLabs. See [The HeatCaller lap voice](online/index.md#the-heatcaller-lap-voice).
    - Lap times from 3.00 to 19.99 seconds, laps 1 to 200, places 1 to 30, gaps up to 30 seconds or 9 laps, and up to 2 seconds under or off a target lap. Anything else, or a clip that's slow to arrive, and the phone's own voice says the whole line, as before.
    - **Phone voice** on both pages switches back to the phone's own voice.
    - Make the clips once with `lap-voice-render.cmd` in the relay folder, on your own ElevenLabs key (about 37,000 characters, roughly $1.50), then redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). Until then the phone's voice reads every lap. Nothing changes on the race PC.
- **Lap times in my ear on a driver's own night (29 October).** The **Right now** card on a driver's own night page has **Read out my laps**: their phone reads out each of their own laps as they race, with no one to pick, and says how the lap went against the target lap race control set them: "Lap 7, 9.42, best lap, 0.2 under target, P2, 0.4 seconds behind". **Time only** gives "9.42, best lap, 0.2 under target". The page finds the driver's own row on the live board under any of their transponders, so a driver who shares a name with somebody else still hears only their own laps. See [Right now](online/index.md#right-now).
    - HeatCaller now sends the gap to the car ahead with every row of the live board, so the **Live** page's lap times say the gap even when the club doesn't show an **Interval** column. Each row also carries a tag that says nothing about the transponder, and each driver's private live details carry the tags of their own cars and their target laps. That's how the night page finds their row. Transponder numbers still never leave the laptop.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC and redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). With an older HeatCaller the night page doesn't show the button.
- **Lap times in my ear on the club's relay pages (29 October).** Follow a driver on the relay's **Live** page and tap **Lap times in my ear**: the phone reads out each of their laps in its own voice, a second or two after the line, on any connection: "Lap 7, 9.42, best lap, P2, 0.4 seconds behind". Tick **Time only** for just "9.42, best lap". The page keeps the screen on while it's talking. It reads only what the live board already shows, so nothing new is published, and it uses the phone's own voice, so there's no cost. See [Lap times in my ear](online/index.md#lap-times-in-my-ear).
    - Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). Nothing changes on the race PC.
- **The club's pages: a bar along the bottom on phones (29 October).** Getting around the relay pages on a phone is easier. See [Getting around](online/index.md#getting-around).
    - **Five buttons, always in reach.** On a phone, **Home**, **Live**, **Schedule**, **Results** and **Me** sit in a bar along the bottom of every page instead of a row of tabs across the top that ran off the screen. **Standings**, **Achievements** and **Tracks** are tiles on Home. On a laptop or tablet every tab is still in one row at the top.
    - **Live dot.** A small red dot on **Live** while a car is on track.
    - **Me.** **My night** is now **Me**, and shows whether or not online check-in is on. A driver's night, check-in and sign-up pages light it up. While online check-in is open, **Me** has **Check in for tonight**; **Check in** no longer has a tab of its own (the Home tile stays).
    - **No more dead ends.** A wrong or old address under a club shows "That page isn't here" with the club's tabs, instead of a line of code. An address with no club says "No club here", and the relay's own address says what it is. The manager page has a **Club page** link.
    - The relay must be redeployed (`fly deploy --ha=false`). Nothing changes on the race PC.
- **Me remembers you (29 October).** Once a driver's night has opened on their phone, from their badge, night email or personal PIN, **Me** goes straight back to it every time, even when online check-in is closed. **Sign out on this phone** at the bottom of the night forgets them. Only the code that opens their night is kept on the phone, never the PIN. A driver who checks in with their personal PIN now gets **See your racing from last time** too, as badge check-ins already did. See [Getting around](online/index.md#getting-around). Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`).
- **Little links on the club's pages (29 October).** A driver's night has **Watch live** on the **Right now** card while their race is on track, and **See the running order** while it's next. On a phone that remembers its driver, Home opens with **Your next race**, with the same links and **Open your night**. **You are in!** after a sign-up has **Go to Me**. See [Getting around](online/index.md#getting-around). Redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`); nothing changes on the race PC.
- **The coach page is on Local Screens (29 October).** A new **Driver coach:** row on **Local Screens** has the coach page's address (`/coach`) with **Copy** and **QR code** buttons, like the other phone pages. Its poster reads **DRIVER COACH**: "Scan, pick your driver, tick Say my lap time." Before, the page wasn't listed and you had to type `/coach` onto the live results address. See [The addresses](screens/phone-and-web.md#the-addresses).
- **The time left, called through a timed race (28 October).** Two new rows on **Voice → Announcements**, beside **Final minute**: **Minutes left** ("Four minutes to go") and **Last minute countdown** (45, 30 and 15 seconds). Once ticked, **Minutes left** calls the last five minutes; set how many with **Only the last** _n_ **minutes**, or untick it to hear every minute. Both are off to start with: tick them to hear them. A time call that had to wait behind another line is only said while it's still true. See [Every announcement](announcer/voice.md#every-announcement).
- **My night, easier to find your way round (28 October).** A driver's own night page on the club's relay pages is laid out so they find what they want in a tap or two. See [Finding your way round](online/index.md#finding-your-way-round).
    - **Jump bar.** Buttons under their name go straight to **Races**, **Report card**, **Driver score**, **Progress**, **Race calls** and **Your details**, and stay at the top as they scroll. A night of two cars has a button for each car.
    - **At a glance.** Their best finish and best lap (with the race each came in), races and laps, above the night's facts.
    - **One race open at a time.** **Your races** is now a short list, one row per race with where it left them, its laps and a **Penalty** tag. Their latest race opens by itself; tap another to open it. The open race has **Lap stats**, **Lap times** and **Lap chart** on tabs, with four stats first and the rest under **More stats**.
    - **Line the races up.** Buttons over the list show **Best lap**, **Avg. lap**, **Top 3 consec.** or **Consistency** on every row, the night's best in green.
    - **Driver score.** Under the report card: their driver rating out of 150, the average and best over the night's races with a bar for each. The laptop now sends each race's rating with the night. See [Driver score](online/index.md#driver-score).
    - **Tidier around it.** On **Right now**, check-in and the transponder sit side by side and requests to the desk fold to a count. The share and save buttons sit side by side. In **Your details** the battery pack comes first and the request to change how they're called is folded under it.
    - Update HeatCaller on the race PC for the driver score, and redeploy the relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`) for the new layout.
- **Results like LiveRC's, and every lap on a driver's night (28 October).**
    - **Heat results.** Each result on the club's relay pages now shows the race length ("3:00 Timed" or "20 Laps"), and for every driver their grid slot under **Qual**, their laps and time ("20/3:10.513"), how far **Behind** the car in front they were (a time, or "2 Laps"), and their best lap.
    - **View laps.** Under each driver, **View laps** opens their lap stats and every lap with its pace ("18/3:02.034") and where they were running, best lap highlighted.
    - **Lap-by-lap position graph.** Under each race, a line per driver shows where they ran after every lap, starting from their grid slot.
    - **My night.** Each race card on a driver's own night now has their **Lap stats** (fast lap, average, average of their top 5, 10 and 15, best 2 and 3 laps in a row, standard deviation and consistency) and **Lap times** with pace and position, under the lap chart.
    - Races filed before this version show as before, with no laps to view.
    - The relay must be redeployed for these changes.
- **Podium videos stay on the laptop (28 October).** The top three's highlight videos are no longer put on the club's relay pages. They're still made after every race and kept in the night's **Highlights** folder on the race PC, exactly as before; **Share video** in a driver's drawer on Race Control still opens theirs.
    - The Big Screen no longer shows the **PODIUM VIDEOS** QR code after a race.
    - The relay pages no longer have a **Videos** tab or a **Podium videos** tile, a driver's night page no longer lists **Your podium videos**, and the night email no longer mentions one. An old QR code or link to a video now opens the relay's "not found".
    - The tick box *Put the podium's videos on the club's page, with a QR code on the Big Screen to download them* is gone from **Club Website → Recording**. Drivers' own race calls on their night page (**Put each driver's clip on their own night on the club's page**) are unchanged.
    - Redeploy your relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`). On its first start it deletes the podium videos it was still holding, so the space they took on the volume (up to 1 GB a club) comes back.
- **The license agreement and privacy notice, accepted before HeatCaller is used (28 October).** The installer is now **HeatCallerApp-beta.msi**, and it opens on the beta license agreement and the privacy notice: it won't install until **I accept** is ticked. HeatCaller itself shows the same text the first time it starts, in full in a box that scrolls, with a tick for each part. The ticks stay greyed out until the text has been scrolled to the end, and HeatCaller won't open until both are ticked. **Decline** closes it. It records when they were accepted, and by which Windows user, and asks again whenever the terms change, so a club that installed with **Setup.exe** is asked too. The privacy notice says what HeatCaller keeps, what goes to the club's online pages, and that the announcer's lines, with drivers' names in them, go to ElevenLabs when you use an online voice. The agreement is between your club and HeatCaller LLC, which makes HeatCaller. Read them again any time from **Backup & Restore → Getting help → License and Privacy...**, or from **About HeatCaller**. These are beta terms, and will be revised before HeatCaller is generally released. See [Install](getting-started/install.md#install).
- **Bug fixes from race night, and drivers' own nights by PIN (26 October).**
    - **Heat reminders through Listen Along.** Reminders between races could be dropped before they reached the relay, or lost on a phone whose download failed once. Listen lines now go to the relay first, a failed line is tried again, and a stuck request no longer stops the player. A short car number said once no longer holds back later lines that contain the same number ("5 minutes to Heat 2").
    - **Live captions.** Long lines - the whole recap, the intros - now come through in short pieces as they're spoken, so the relay's captions and the Big Screen keep moving instead of freezing on one cut-off line. Captions never show words the room didn't hear.
    - **Cast hand-overs.** The throw from the host to a cast member, and the hand-back, are now written by the AI - a line or two that follows on from what was just said - and voiced ahead. The hand-back names the host. If the AI isn't ready in time, a built-in line that also names the host is used.
    - **No pauses after the flag.** The unofficial result, the colour lines, tonight's recap and a driver's night are now voiced ahead, piece by piece, so they play back to back. Reset or Mute stops them.
    - **Check-in needs the driver's own PIN.** On the track Wi-Fi page and on the relay, checking a driver in or out from a phone now needs that driver's personal PIN. Staff still check anyone in from the full list with the staff PIN. See [Check-in](online/check-in.md).
    - **My night.** Drivers can open their own night page on the club's relay pages with their personal PIN. See [Opening your night with your PIN](online/index.md).
    - **Podium videos.** A new **Videos** tab on the club's relay pages lists the week's podium videos with downloads for 1st, 2nd and 3rd.
    - **Music under the videos.** The highlight and podium videos now carry the music and crowd the room heard. Untick **Put the music and crowd under the highlight videos** on **Club Website → Recording** if your music isn't licensed for posting online.
    - **Recording** has moved from **Sounds** to **Club Website**.
    - The relay must be redeployed for the Listen Along, captions, check-in, **My night** and **Videos** changes.
- **Race Control: a steady flag row, and taller meters (26 October).** From a cold start the row is **Stage**, **Countdown**, **Green** and **Reset**, centred over the On Air bar, and it stays that way through On Air, a warm-up, staging and the countdown. Once the race is green it's **Green**, **Yellow**, **Red**, **Checkered** and **Reset** until the flag. After the flag it's **Next Heat**, **Results**, **Edit Result** and **Reset** as before, and **Reset** goes back to the first set. The level meters now run from the top of the card's **RACE CONTROL** title to the foot of the On Air bar, which stops just short of them, and they're gone from the **Broadcast** window. The On Air bar names the recap show by the name typed on the **AI Commentary** page, when there is one. Double-click the On Air bar to open the **Broadcast** window. See [The flag row](race-control/running-a-race.md#the-flag-row).
- **Race Control: the On Air bar stays up, and Reset stops everything (26 October).** The line under the flag row is now the **On Air bar** whenever anything is going out. It names the segment on air (**On air: Race Recap**) instead of "the show is going out", with the jingle, bed or music playing shown inside it rather than off at the far right. Holding Red, Checkered or Reset works as before. **Reset** now stops everything going out at once: the announcer mid-sentence, everything queued, the recap show, a replay, every jingle and bed, the sponsor spot on the Big Screen, the music and the crowd. The word for where the race is (READY, FINISHED...) is gone from the left of the row, so the buttons and meters fit on one line and the Race Control card is shorter. See [The On Air bar](race-control/running-a-race.md#the-on-air-bar).
- **Race Control: level meters on the flag row, and Now playing under it (26 October).** The **VOICE**, **MUSIC** and **CROWD** strips now have a fourth, **SFX**, so the announcer and the sound effects, lap beeps and imaging show separately. All four are at the right of Race Control's flag row, where **Now playing** was, as well as in the **Broadcast** window. **Now playing** has moved to the right end of the line under the row, where On Air's bar is. See [Level meters](race-control/running-a-race.md#level-meters).
- **Broadcast window: channel strips (25 October).** Beside what's on air there are now three level meters, like a radio board's: **VOICE** (the announcer, effects and imaging), **MUSIC** and **CROWD**, each with left and right bars that move smoothly like a VU meter, a peak line and green, amber and red zones. They move in time with what the room hears. All the buttons are where they were: **Hold**, **Play again**, **Replay Moment**, **Skip**, **Notes**, **Votes** and the lines-not-said count. See [The Broadcast window](announcer/commentary.md#the-broadcast-window).
- **Race Control: On Air's wait on the strip, not a button (25 October).** The **On Air** button is gone from the flag row. While the opening is written and voiced, the strip under the row shows it the way it shows a press and hold - "On Air: voicing the opening…", a bar filling, and "about 8s · press O to skip to Stage" - and it turns cyan while the show is going out. <kbd>F1</kbd>, **Start Next** and the other starts still go through On Air, and <kbd>O</kbd> starts or skips it. See [On Air](race-control/running-a-race.md#on-air).
- **Race Control: the row after the flag (25 October).** Once a race is finished the flag row shows **Next Heat**, **Results**, **Edit Result** and **Reset**, as in the Race Control mockup. **Next Heat** does what **Start Next** does (or **Stage** is there, with nothing next in the program); **Results** and **Edit Result** open the race just run in **Race History**. See [The flag row](race-control/running-a-race.md#the-flag-row).
- **Voice → Cast: one Load Voices, at the top (25 October).** The **Voices** card is now first on the Cast tab, and its **Load Voices** fills every voice list on the tab, the announcer's **ElevenLabs voice:** included. The second **Load Voices** that sat beside the announcer's ElevenLabs voice is gone.
- **Voice → Cast: every card in the same order (25 October).** Each speaker's card now starts with their name, then their voice and **Test Voice**, then what they do and their settings - the announcer, recap host, co-host, Technical Lead, Team Principal and achievements reader alike. The reader's name box is now captioned **Reader's name:**.
- **Voice → Cast: a Kokoro voice for anyone, whatever the announcer uses (25 October).** Every voice list on the Cast tab — recap host, co-host, Technical Lead, Team Principal and achievements reader — now ends with the Kokoro voices on this PC, marked "Kokoro:". Pick one and that speaker talks in Kokoro's free voice even beside an ElevenLabs or HeatCaller announcer; **Test Voice** beside each list plays it. See [Use the Kokoro voice](announcer/voice.md#use-the-kokoro-voice).
- **Race Control: the announcer's buttons in a window of their own (25 October).** **Hold**, **Play again**, **Replay Moment**, **Skip**, **Notes** and **Votes** have moved from the row under the Next Up bar into the **Broadcast** window. Open it with the new **Broadcast** button on Race Control, under **Mission Control**. Across its top is what's on air or went out last; under the buttons, **At a glance** says what the announcer is doing, tonight's line budget, this race's timing calls, the notes waiting, the votes in, and whether the recap show or a replay moment is ready. Closing the window only hides it, so a hold stays on: the **Broadcast** button reads **Broadcast · HELD** in amber while it does, and **Broadcast · 3 notes** when notes are waiting. **Open Big Screen** and **Open Announcer Screen** on Race Control are now **Big Screen** and **Announcer Screen**. See [The Broadcast window](announcer/commentary.md#the-broadcast-window).
- **Race Control: a flag row that follows the race (25 October).** Only the buttons that mean something now are on the row: **On Air**, **Stage**, **Countdown**, **Green** and **Reset** between races; **Yellow**, **Red**, **Checkered** and **Reset** while racing, with **Green** back under a yellow or red. A word at the left says where the race is. The function keys and the phone remote still reach everything. See [The flag row](race-control/running-a-race.md#the-flag-row).
- **Race Control: press and hold Red, Checkered and Reset (25 October).** A click on them no longer acts: hold the button for about a second. A clock beside the caption marks them, and the line under the row shows "Keep holding to throw the red flag…" with a bar filling and "let go to cancel". Let go early and nothing happens. **Yellow** is still one click, and <kbd>F5</kbd>, <kbd>F6</kbd> and <kbd>F7</kbd> work as before. See [Press and hold](race-control/running-a-race.md#press-and-hold).
- **Race Control: Now playing (25 October).** The right of the flag row names, in cyan, the jingle, bed, sweeper, bumper, stinger, sponsor video, music or crowd going out right now, and is blank when nothing is. See [The On Air bar](race-control/running-a-race.md#the-on-air-bar), where it is now.
- **A driver's own evening, on their phone (22 Oct 2026).** Drivers get much more from their own night page, the Wi-Fi follow view and the coach page, and can ask the desk for changes without queuing. Everything that goes online is off until you tick it. See [A driver's own night](online/index.md#a-drivers-own-night).
    - **Right now** at the top of the night page: their next race and grid slot, about when it should start ("about 7 min (19:42)"), **Turn on alerts** to beep and buzz when their race is next and again when it's staged, their marshal duty, a **Check in** button, and **Check my transponder** to see that the timing line hears them.
    - **A card for each race**: how it went, a lap chart, any penalties with race control's reason, and the announcer's call of that race to play or download (with **Put each driver's clip on their own night on the club's page** on **Sounds → Recording**). Then their improvement tonight, their best lap over the last six nights, and badges within reach.
    - **A first-night guide** for a new driver, on their night page and their check-in card.
    - **Driver requests**: from **Your details** on the night page a driver can ask to change their announcer name, pronouns, a line about them for the commentary, their email (with their personal PIN), their battery pack, or their online privacy, and can query a result with **Something wrong?**. The desk answers in the new **Driver Requests** window on **Race Day → Who's Racing**, with a tick per change and **Hear It** for a new announcer name. A query is noted in the race's notes and never changes a result. See [Driver requests](online/driver-requests.md).
    - **Online privacy** on each driver profile: **Leave their photo out**, **No podium videos of them** and **Initials only**, for the club's online pages only. A driver can ask for them from their phone; only the desk can turn one off. See [Online privacy](race-day/drivers-and-classes.md#online-privacy).
    - **Their night by email**: **Offer drivers their night by email** on the Club Website page, with **Email Tonight's Nights** or an automatic send when the race day closes, for drivers who ticked **Email them their night**. See [Drivers' nights by email](online/setup.md#drivers-nights-by-email).
    - **Captions**: the announcer's words as text on the club's live page (**Captions on the club's live page**), on the Big Screen (press <kbd>C</kbd>), and on the Wi-Fi pages and stream with `?captions=1`. See [Captions](online/index.md#captions).
    - **Penalties explained**: the Wi-Fi follow view shows a driver's warnings and penalties with the reason, and the public results show a "+5s" or "1 warning" chip, numbers only.
    - **On the Wi-Fi follow view**: a "STAGED - GO TO THE DRIVER STAND" call-up with a second buzz, about when the race should start, and **Check my transponder**. See [Follow a driver](screens/phone-and-web.md#follow-a-driver).
    - **A pit board** on the coach page: last lap, position, laps, gap ahead and gap to target, full screen, keeping the phone awake. See [The pit board](screens/phone-and-web.md#the-pit-board).
    - **Two cars on one night**: a driver who raced a second transponder as a car of its own now finds those races on their own night page, in a section of their own ("Second car · RC10 B7") with its own report card, improvement and progress, instead of on a page nobody could open. The night email does the same, and goes to them once. See [Two cars on one night](online/index.md#two-cars-on-one-night).
    - **Club admins: redeploy the relay before switching any of this on.** The relay has changes for these features. Get the new relay code, then from the relay folder run `npm test`, then `fly deploy --ha=false`. See [Updating the relay](admin/relay-hosting.md#updating-the-relay). Until then the laptop reports that the relay needs redeploying, and the night page looks as it did before.
- **Tidier pages throughout.** Every page's fields now line up in one column down the page (Imaging, Ambience, Season rules, Sponsors, Club Website, Local Screens, Venues and the Voice tab had cards out of line), a few drop-downs that could cut their text short now size to it, and the gray hint text has been shortened and de-duplicated everywhere - nothing a setting does has changed.
- **The announcer's voice is picked on the Cast tab.** The voice picker and **Test Voice** for whichever voice engine you use are now at the top of **Voice → Cast**, in the Announcer card, beside everyone else on the show. The Voice tab still chooses what speaks (HeatCaller, ElevenLabs or Kokoro), the key and the tuning; Kokoro's Download stays there.
- **The AI Commentary page, tidied.** The cards are in the order the night happens - **Who writes it**, **Before the start**, **Through the night**, **After the flag**, **Recap show**, **Writing style** - with about half the gray hint text and no repeats. Every setting is still there and works the same; the qualifying recap now sits under the driver introductions it opens, and the pit-lane throw is listed after them, as On Air says them. See [Settings](announcer/commentary.md#settings).

- **Full names from the announcer again, if you want them.** Since **Shorten published names** started reaching the HeatCaller voice and ElevenLabs, the announcer has said "Sam K" on those voices. Tick the new **Say full names aloud with the online voices**, under it on the Club Website page, to hear full names while the club's pages and captions keep the short ones. A line that says a full name isn't sent to people listening along at home. See [Shortened names on the paid voices](announcer/voice.md#fix-a-line-that-came-out-wrong).
- **HeatCaller has its own icon.** The Sunset Grid (a synthwave sun over a pink grid) is now on the desktop and Start menu shortcuts, the taskbar, every window and the installer. HeatCaller also puts it in the system tray by the clock while it runs: double-click it, or right-click and choose **Show HeatCaller**, to bring the window to the front; **Exit** closes HeatCaller the same as the window's X, asking first if a race is running. The rehearsal copy's tray icon says "HeatCaller Rehearsal".
- **Track Designer: tiles that lock together like the real ones (25 October).** Each RCP tile is drawn with the seven T-shaped teeth it has on every edge, measured off a real 50 cm tile. A tooth is a narrow neck with a wider head that hooks under the next tile's, so each joint is a fine line stepping round the teeth. The outside edge of the floor shows its bare teeth with the floor between them, and the floor's bottom-right outside corners are clipped the way a real tile's corner is. Before, every edge had one round tab that spilled over the tile beside it, and each tile had four dots no real tile has. Zoomed out, the joints are a plain line. The track's picture, Save as PNG and the club's track page show the same.
- **Track Designer: a new look (25 October).** The drawing gets much more of the window. The palette that ran down the left is now the **Pieces** tab of the one panel on the right, which has five tabs: **Pieces**, **Plan** (start from a kit, the track's name, the tile size, the kit check), **Room** (the room and the traced photo), **Lap** and **Keys** (every key the designer answers to). It opens on the tab you last used. **Panel** on the toolbar, or <kbd>P</kbd>, hides the panel so the drawing takes the whole width. The drawing sits on a dark floor whatever HeatCaller's look, with the plan's size and the lap length (and the layout's average lap time, when it has one) in its top-left corner. Hold <kbd>Space</kbd> and drag to move the view with any tool. **Save as PNG** moved into a new **Export** menu on the toolbar.
- **Track Designer: draw with lanes (25 October).** A new **Lane** tool (<kbd>L</kbd>): drag where the track goes, one, two or three tiles wide. Floor goes down under it, rails go down both sides by themselves, and turn rails go round the outside of every bend. Change the lane and its rails follow it: start on a lane to widen a bend or add a bay. Two lanes side by side get a divider between them. **Ring** lays a whole closed lane round a box you drag. **Turns round the bends** sets one-tile turns, two-tile turns or square corners for every lane on the plan. A new **Corners** tool (<kbd>C</kbd>) clicks any corner through a one-tile turn, a two-tile turn and square. Rails you put down by hand are never moved, and plans drawn before lanes are left exactly as they were. See [Draw with lanes](race-day/track-designer.md#draw-with-lanes).
- **Track Designer: Trace route on wide tracks (25 October).** **Trace route** finds the middle of a track two or three tiles wide; before, it stopped at the first tile open on three sides. A traced line now follows the track as you change it, until you move a point of it by hand. It keeps running the way round it was going, so **Reverse** (now also <kbd>Shift</kbd>+<kbd>R</kbd>) stays reversed. Turn rails also keep the line in now: with floor laid beyond a corner, the line used to step out through the turn. On a one-tile track, the traced line is the same as before.
- **Track Designer: the lap time, always on screen (25 October).** The lap in the corner of the drawing now always has a time and says where it's from: **from N laps raced** on that layout (new: worked out from your saved races), **as entered** on Track Layouts, or an **estimate** from the plan for a layout that's never been raced or whose line you've changed. The estimate tunes itself to your club's real laps. **Play** drives at that time, at half speed, as timed or double (Lap tab), showing the lap and speed. Click the lap to see the sectors. See [The lap time](race-day/track-designer.md#the-lap-time).
- **Track Designer: print to scale, and share a plan (25 October).** **Export** now has **Save picture** at four sizes (Small to Poster, each saying how big it comes out), **Copy picture**, **Print track and parts list...** and **Save plan file...**. The print is to scale - 1:10, 1:20, 1:25, 1:50 or fit on one sheet - over as many sheets as it takes, each labeled with where it goes, lettered and numbered along its edges, with a scale bar that says how long it should measure. After the plan come the parts it takes (against what the club owns, when that's entered) and the order to build it in: the floor row by row, the rails, the dividers, the turns and the markings, each named by the grid on the sheets. A **.hctrack** plan file holds the whole plan for another club to open with **Open plan file...** on the **Plan** tab; <kbd>Ctrl</kbd>+<kbd>Z</kbd> puts your own back. See [Print and share a plan](race-day/track-designer.md#print-and-share-a-plan).
- **Track Designer: stretch, name, and see it in 3D (25 October).** Pick part of the plan up with **Select** and drag the square handle on its right or bottom edge to make a straight longer or shorter by whole tiles. The stretch runs right across the plan, so both straights of an oval grow together, and the lanes' rails follow. **Tile label** on the **Markings** card (or <kbd>T</kbd>) names a tile, for example "Pit lane", and the build list prints it. **View** (or <kbd>V</kbd>) shows the track tipped and turned, in 3D: the floor, the rails standing up with their shadows, the markings and the racing line, with the car going round while **Play** runs. Drag to turn and tip it, and use the wheel to zoom. The Track Designer now has a page of its own. See [Track Designer](race-day/track-designer.md).
- **Track Designer: Build me a track (25 October).** On the **Plan** tab, **Build me a track...** offers layouts made to fit the room you've entered: RCP's own that fit, then an oval, an L, a U hairpin, a T, an S-bend and a serpentine as big as the room allows, longest lap first, each within what the club owns. Pick one and it's on the board with its finish line and racing line, made of lanes so you can reshape it straight away. See [Build me a track](race-day/track-designer.md#build-me-a-track).
- **Track Designer: what the club owns (25 October).** A new **Stock** tab checks the plan against your club's own track. Enter your boxed sets and loose parts once (**Edit what we own...**) and each part shows as used, owned and left, in red when you're short; the plan's size in the corner of the drawing turns red too. Corner tiles are now counted: each bend takes an outer curve tile and an inner corner tile, and **Check against a kit** catches a plan with more bends than the box has corner tiles for. The Lane tool now starts two tiles wide, as real RCP lanes are. See [What the club owns](race-day/track-designer.md#what-the-club-owns).
- **Track Designer: easier drawing (25 October).** Before you click, the drawing shows what the click will make, in orange, and rings the seam or corner it will land on. The zoom shows in the drawing's bottom-right corner; click it to go back to 100%. **Wall the edge** no longer puts a rail under a turn. Fixed: a plan with two-tile turns at its corners was measured two tiles too big on every side, so the size in the corner of the drawing, **Fit** and the saved picture all framed empty space round it.

- **Timing in a layer of its own (23 October).** Timing no longer depends on the window. The lap counting, race clock, finish, crash-recovery file and race filing now sit apart from the screens, in a part of HeatCaller that can't touch them. See [Run timing on its own thread](timing/decoders.md#run-timing-on-its-own-thread).
  - **Passes are judged by when they arrived.** If the window was busy across the time limit, a car that crossed before the limit still counts as before it. Before, it could be finished a lap early.
  - **Run timing on its own thread** on the Connection page (off to begin with; takes effect after a restart). With it ticked, a window that freezes can't hold up laps, the time limit, the checkered flag, the start or the crash-recovery file, and stale lap calls aren't spoken after a freeze.
  - **The race is filed and its crash-recovery file is kept** even when the Race Control page isn't open.
  - **The last pieces moved off the window (24 October).** With **Run timing on its own thread** ticked, split-loop passes go straight to timing (a freeze no longer loses sector times), an automatic start's green drops as soon as the countdown audio ends (cars can't be booked as jump starts because the window was late), phones keep the board live while the window is frozen, and the crash-recovery file is still written while an earlier unsaved race waits to be filed.
  - **No practice at the gate during On Air.** A car warming up while On Air's opening plays no longer starts practice and calls On Air off, whether or not timing is on its own thread.
  - **Record every pass to a file** on the Connection page keeps every pass and race command, with when it arrived, so a night can be checked afterwards. See [Record every pass to a file](timing/decoders.md#record-every-pass-to-a-file).

- **Whole-app bug hunt (23 October): 107 fixes.** Every part of HeatCaller and the club relay was reviewed. Timing itself (laps, places, points) came out clean. The ones a club will notice:
  - **Privacy online:**
    - A car with no name, said aloud by its transponder number, no longer puts that number on the club's page. This covers captions, listen-along, podium videos and the AI commentary sent to the relay.
    - Listen-along now follows **Shorten published names**.
    - A driver who chose **Initials only** stays initials in the standings, the results archive and the tracks page, even under a name they raced with before a rename. They also stay initials if their profile is deleted, and in a night-before preview already on the page.
    - Captions burned into podium videos leave out race-control calls and initials-only names. A video whose sound says one of those stays on the PC.
    - A podium video that was still uploading when the driver chose **No podium videos of them** is dropped.
    - Rental transponders no longer get a driver night page that the next renter would see.
    - The staff check-in list no longer hands out drivers' private badge codes.
  - **Driver requests:** a second change sent before the desk answers no longer throws away the first one (a "no photo online" tick, say). The phone shows a driver's newest answers. Undoing an approved pack change sets the request back to waiting. The 10-queries-a-night limit runs 6am to 6am. One venue Wi-Fi address no longer blocks the whole paddock after 20 requests.
  - **Nothing of the club's is deleted:** clearing old race recordings in a club-chosen folder only removes HeatCaller's own files, and a rehearsal records into its own folder. A race-in-progress file that can't be read at start-up is kept, not deleted. Notes and the room vote survive looking at a past race day. A query noted into a race's notes is no longer wiped by Race History. Night emails and PIN emails already sent are recorded even if HeatCaller is closed mid-send, so nobody gets them twice.
  - **On race night:**
    - **Check my transponder** reports the pass just made, not the evening's average.
    - The coach page no longer says a lap time twice on weak Wi-Fi. It numbers laps like the board, and **Say my lap time** resumes with one tap after a reload.
    - A heat goes back to unrun on the timetable when its race is deleted.
    - The AI commentary waits for every qualifying heat before saying who has pole.
    - Show music stops when it should.
    - The live page's checkered flag shows its color.
    - The night page's lap chart leaves out the start lap.
  - **Spending:** talking points respect the commentary budget's reserve. A relay timeout is no longer retried eight times at a cost each time. Intros aren't voiced ahead in ElevenLabs when **Start races** won't use them. Sponsors are credited only for lines actually heard, and get their place back when a line is dropped.
  - **Phone pages keep trying:** the schedule, sign-up, results and night pages retry after a busy or failed answer from the relay instead of saying there's nothing there.
  - **Smaller ones:** a duplicated tech-inspection item keeps its FAIL. Track Designer remembers whether the plan is the main picture. A Quick Race's name no longer sticks to the next race. The Rentals badge follows online sign-up approvals. Several "per night" counts now use the 6am race-night cutoff instead of midnight. Empty or broken settings files load safely.

- **Bug hunt over the last three days' work (20 to 22 October): 41 fixes.** The ones a club will notice:
  - **Podium videos online:** the relay no longer cuts a video upload off after 30 seconds, so a podium video sent over an ordinary club connection arrives (a stalled upload is still dropped after 30 seconds of silence). Videos older than a week are now cleared every hour, not only when the club uploads again. **Delete everything** now stops an upload already on its way, so it can't put a video back after the wipe. A shared videos link now opens that race's videos.
  - **Names in videos:** a video that goes online now follows **Abbreviate names** for every driver in it, and a car with no name shows "Unnamed driver" instead of its transponder number.
  - **Highlight videos:** they're made one at a time, and wait while a race is on track, so the PC isn't encoding behind a live race. A PC without the video encoder stops trying after the first refusal.
  - **Personal bests:** a main no longer calls a "personal best" the driver already beat in a heat earlier that night.
  - **On Air:** the next race's opening no longer reads out the last race's held-back badges. A line voiced ahead while ElevenLabs was down is voiced again in the real voice once it's back.
  - **Coaching:** the gap to the ghost now allows for time penalties, qualifying clocks and staggered releases. The phone coach page speaks the first lap of every race (not only the first race), and its Target and Say my lap time rows hide properly. A report card for a past night uses that night's cars and setups, and its saved file is named with that night's date. Undoing **Bring their best up to date** on a ghost now really puts it back.
  - **Sound:** **Stop Everything** now stops a crowd that **Play Everything** or an audition started over On Air's silent crowd. **Replay the moment** respects the announcer mute. Replay no longer plays the previous race's audio, and it keeps filling after a recording stops partway through.
  - **Race Control:** pressing a lap-breakdown drawer button twice quickly counts both presses, and a double-click is only taken for the driver it was meant for.
  - **Smaller ones:** the director's own **Coming up next** segment no longer gets show music the club didn't tick. Pasted tables with damaged accents are repaired far more often. Undoing both sidebar groupings in a row keeps Race Day and Race Control where they were.

- **Say my lap time on the coach page.** The phone coach page (`/coach`) has a **Say my lap time** tick: the phone reads out that driver's own lap time after every lap ("nine point two one", and "best lap" on a new best), and nobody else's, in the phone's own voice so it never waits for the PA. Remembered on the phone. See [Coach](screens/phone-and-web.md#coach).
- **A new best beeps on an iPhone.** iPhones can't buzz for a web page, so the coach page now beeps there instead, once the page has been tapped.
- **Chase their best from the board.** Race Control's **Coach *name*** menu now has **Chase *name*'s best (ghost)**, the same own-best ghost as the Practice page's **Chase a Best Lap...**.
- **Gap to their ghost.** **Coach *name*** → **Gap to their ghost** (once they have an own-best ghost running) has the announcer say where the ghost is on the track as the driver crosses the line: "Two tenths behind your ghost.", "A tenth ahead of your ghost." It's the new **Gap to their ghost (coach)** line on **Voice → Announcements**, on in practice and off in races until ticked.
- **The ghost sits beside its driver.** In practice, an own-best ghost's row is now just under the driver it chases on Race Control's board, so the two are read together. Races are untouched.
- **Driver rating on the report card.** The report card now gives the driver rating (out of 150) each race earned that night, or the average over several races and the best.
- **Report cards print.** A report card now opens in its own window with **Print...**, **Save Image...** and **Copy**, so it can go to the driver on paper or be sent on.

## 0.60.0

Released 22 Oct 2026. Everything since 0.59.0, newest first.

- **Coaching (22 Oct).** Ways to coach a driver while they practise, and to show them how their night went. See [Coaching a driver](race-control/running-a-race.md#coaching-a-driver).
  - **Coach *name*** on Race Control's right-click: **At the split**, **Target lap...**, **Consistency** and **Tones instead of words**, for one driver at a time.
  - **Target lap**: set a target and they hear each lap against it, "On target.", "Two tenths off." or "A tenth under." The target stays with the driver for the night.
  - **Consistency**: every five clean laps (adjustable), how far apart they were, "Last five within a tenth - that's your rhythm.", and whether that's tighter or looser than before.
  - **Tones instead of words**: a short, quiet tone at the line and the split. High is quicker, low is slower, and two rising notes mean a new best. Tones play the moment the car crosses, never queued behind the announcer. The consistency line is still spoken, since a tone can't say a spread.
  - The two new lines, **Target lap (coach)** and **Consistency (coach)**, are on in practice and off in races until you tick them on **Voice → Announcements**.
  - **Coach page on phones**: `/coach` on the club's Wi-Fi shows one driver's laps as they come in, the last lap against their best and their target, their last ten laps, a flash and buzz on a new best, and their report card. See [Coach](screens/phone-and-web.md#coach).
  - **Report card**: a few lines of arithmetic about a driver's night (best and ideal lap, consistency, the sector that costs most, against last time on the layout, what tonight's setup change did, and the gap to the quickest driver they raced with). It's on the coach page, on the driver's own night page online, and in Race History's and Driver Hub's right-click (**Report Card for *name*...**). The setup line is only on their own night page and on the club's laptop, and names online are shortened as your public pages are. The online card needs the relay redeployed (`fly deploy --ha=false`).
  - **Chase a Best Lap...** on the Practice page's ghost cars card: a ghost of a driver's own best lap, "Sam's best", at their best tonight on the layout (or ever, before tonight's). Pick the same driver again to bring it up to their new best. A new ghost pace source, **A driver's best tonight (or ever, before tonight's)**, does the same in the ghost editor. See [Chase your own best](race-control/practice-and-ghosts.md#chase-your-own-best).
- **Hearing the splits (22 Oct).** With a split loop, the announcer can now read the split loop's times. See [Hearing the splits](timing/decoders.md#hearing-the-splits).
  - **Split time** reads each car's time to the split as it crosses, "Eight point four zero seconds at the split.", with **Read every** _n_ **split crossing(s) per driver** to space it out.
  - **Coach *name*** → **At the split**, on Race Control's right-click: pick one driver and they hear "Up a tenth", "Down two tenths" or "Level" against their own best lap every time they cross the split.
  - **Battle gap at the split** times a close fight at the split: "At the split, Sam's two tenths up on Bill."
  - These three are on in practice and off in races until you tick them.
  - **Sectors at the flag** (off until you tick it) adds who was quickest through each sector to the result at the flag: "Quickest through sector one was Sam, sector two Bill."
- **Printing, a mark for laps counted by hand, and more (22 Oct).**
  - **Program tab:** **Print Heat Sheet...** prints a class's heat sheet straight to a printer, with a preview first. **Save Heat Sheet...** still saves PNGs.
  - **Race History:** **Print Results...** prints the selected races (Ctrl or Shift to pick several) on letter-size pages for the pit board, with a preview first.
  - **Laps counted by hand are marked.** A lap count that includes laps added with **+1 Lap** during the race, or in **Edit Result** afterwards, shows as "12\*" on the board, in Race History, the Event Report, printed results and the **Live Results** page, with "\* includes laps counted by hand" (not yet on the club's website). The lap strip shows "+N by hand", and a new optional **By Hand** column shows the count. Scoring is unchanged. The CSV and JSON exports gain a laps-counted-by-hand column or field, added last so sheets built on the old columns still line up.
  - **Driver Profiles:** **Paste a Table...** imports drivers from a list copied off your old timing software's web page or out of a spreadsheet, with the same column preview, matching and undo as **Import from CSV**.
  - **Race Control:** **Replay Moment** on the announcer row plays the last 20 seconds the room heard again (the call, the crowd and the music), marked in and out with the show's sting; **Skip** stops it. It needs race recording on.
  - **Voice Settings:** **Livelier in the big races, steadier in the heats** (on by default, your own ElevenLabs voice, models other than v3): a touch steadier in practice and heats, a touch livelier in the top main and the last race of the night. Lines you've already saved or prepared are still played and never bought again.
  - **AI commentary** is written to be heard: contractions, and short commentator fragments where they fit.
  - **For beta clubs:** a tick in **Manage Navigation**, offered at the end of first-time setup, keeps pages not yet tested at a real event (Championship, Garage, Handicaps, Rentals, Venues) out of the sidebar. A search still finds them under **NOT YET TESTED**. Existing installs are unchanged: the option starts off.
  - **Relay:** `mint-club.cmd` adds a club or replaces a token in one step (build, mint, send the config to Fly); `backup.cmd` downloads the whole relay volume to a dated folder; a scheduled GitHub check emails you when the relay stops answering. The relay docs gain a checklist for setting up a club, safe ways to hand over a token, backups and restoring, and when to scale up.
  - **Docs:** the first race night guide has a fallback plan and who to call.

- **Every known issue fixed.** The open list in the known-issues file is down to the installer's download page:
  - **Championship:** a **Points** value can't be set below 0, and a negative row from an older file scores 0, so racing a round can only ever add to a driver's total.
  - **Commentary page:** a new **Segments run:** choice, under **Pacing:**, sets how far down the segment list the night goes (*Everything that is ticked*, *All but the top and tail*, *The live ones only*, *Intros and recaps only*). Segments past it are grayed out, and a change applies straight away.
  - **Shows know how long they have.** The gap to the next race is worked out from the **Run Sheet**, allowing for how late the night is running, so "about 4 minutes away" can be said and a show is fitted to the gap. After a freeform race, or with no program, a show hands back with "Stay with us." instead of nothing.
  - **A race that earned it gets a bigger show.** A photo finish, or a track record set in the race, gives the recap's music a beat more at each end; a night with a photo finish does the same for the sign-off.
  - **The hand-over follows the ticks:** with nothing ticked under **Which segments get it**, it's read without music.
  - **The commercial break is decided once, at the flag**, so the throw never promises a break that doesn't run.
  - **Voice Settings:** **Re-roll Line...** makes a new take of a saved line, plays it and keeps it for the race - on the HeatCaller voice too, where the new take is your club's own (redeploy your relay first). Each saved line shows what it costs in characters, the allowance keeps itself current every two minutes while the page is open, and **Preview** is capped at 200 characters, as the relay is.
  - **Shortened names reach the paid voices.** With **Shorten published names to a first name and an initial** on (the default), lines sent to the HeatCaller voice or ElevenLabs say "Billy S" rather than "Billy Smith", so a full name never leaves the laptop. Kokoro still says names in full. Untick the box to hear full names on a paid voice.
  - **The announcer:** a close battle held back by **Pacing** waits out the gap instead of being offered and held again at every crossing. Calling the next race stops a recap that's still being turned into speech. After a speaker dropout the voice no longer comes in early over a jingle. The built-in AI model's first load no longer holds a line past its time limit. AI personal-best calls compare against the driver's real best on this layout before tonight.
  - **Sponsors:** the sponsor report keeps tonight's "presented by" credits across a restart.
  - **Room vote:** phone votes are no longer lost when the check-in key is created or reissued while a vote is open.
  - **Phone pages on the Wi-Fi:** achievements no longer vanish for five minutes after another program briefly locks the file.
  - **Track Designer:** cutting part of the racing line leaves the line where it is instead of losing those points.
  - **Big Screen:** the **FASTEST SECTOR** banner picks white or dark lettering to suit your look's color, and ghost cars never get a warning banner.
  - **Docs:** installing is now described around the installer, and driver warnings are documented for the first time.

- **Bug hunt over the week's new work: the fixes a club will notice.** A second review, this time of everything added between 15 and 21 October, found and fixed:
  - **Sponsors:** with the AI commentary switched off, no paid read went out all night - not between races and not in the commercial break. They do again. Unticking **Read at the end of an ad break** now really stops reads in the break (the other box only ever meant reads *outside* it). A long sponsor copy read in parts in the break counts as one read in the sponsor report and the rotation, not three. Existing installs now get **Also put reads on rotation between races** switched off, as new ones do - the old default had been saved into every club's sponsors file, so the change never reached them. A written segment that comes back empty no longer uses up a sponsor's "presented by" for the next three minutes.
  - **This Month and the usage meters:** a relay that couldn't read its own count reported "0 used", so a used-up month looked empty and the commentary stopped holding back. The app now keeps its last real figure. Last month's figure is no longer shown as this month's "used up" after the month turns over and before the relay answers, and an ElevenLabs figure that couldn't be refreshed no longer says "Checked at ...".
  - **Highlight and podium videos:** a winner's video could be cut off before the finish call; it now keeps the finish. A late upload from an earlier race no longer puts its QR code over the next race's results. Pruning old nights now deletes the videos too, instead of keeping every one for ever. Each driver's mp3 clip no longer waits for all the videos to be made. Release calls in staggered starts now get captions.
  - **On Air:** driver introductions are now said even when race intros are off, the intro is for mains only, or the AI's intro doesn't come back. **Yellow** or **Red** during a demo's On Air starts the demo, and between races they no longer quietly call On Air off. **Run as Demo** while another demo is still running now starts. Lines voiced ahead are thrown away when the voice is changed, so the opening is never in the old voice.
  - **Race Control:** double-clicking a driver just below an open drawer now opens their lap breakdown (the drawer shutting had moved the row out from under the pointer), and the second click can no longer press a drawer button by mistake.
  - **After the race:** the unofficial result no longer eats the pause after the flag. Pressing **Skip** or **Hold** after the flag no longer loses the achievements that were held back for the recap - they're called one at a time instead. **Lap times only** now silences the unofficial result too. After a practice session, the next race's lines are written ahead straight away instead of waiting 45 seconds.
  - **Sound:** the mute button now silences jingles, bumpers and beds already playing. A bed starting while the announcer is mid-line now ducks under him. **Preview** on the Ambience page and **Play Everything** on the Mixer play the crowd after On Air has opened it silent, and don't leave it playing afterwards. A lead change is never held back by the pacing gap, whatever weight it has.
  - **The relay:** a phone closing a video part way through no longer leaves the relay waiting for ever and holding memory, and at most two video uploads are taken in at once. The room vote's ballot follows **Abbreviate names**. The drivers' nights switch no longer fills the log every 30 seconds while the relay can't be reached, and listening along no longer flips between "not reachable" and "working again".
  - **Smaller ones:** the **Cast** tab's co-host card is no longer greyed out while the co-host is asking the interviews. **Delete Race Day** counts only the achievements it will really take back, and says milestones are kept. Starting a demo from the **Demo Mode** page that never starts no longer leaves its format on Race Control. Handicaps' recent best and **Suggest** work again for Quick Races. A note sent in from the room while the approval window is open is never missed. A setup with no id in a hand-edited file is given one instead of being lost on the next save. The **Show treatment** card no longer says the club's opener and bed don't play on a soundscape with imaging (they still play everywhere but the race recap), and unticking **Race recaps** keeps the club's music off the recap. The sponsor video preview says when a file failed part way through, instead of "End of video". A stop-set throw that the announcer has already said is no longer said again.

- **The Team Principal hears the question he answers.** The booth interview's first question is the host's longest - the welcome, his name and role, his own words from last time read back to him - and it was being cut to length from the end, so the question itself was lost and the Team Principal answered something the room never heard. The question is now always kept, and the welcome is shortened instead.

- **The State and Flag line is gone from Race Control.** The line under the flag buttons that read *State: Finished · Flag: Checkered* has been taken off - the lit flag button and the board already say both. The flag buttons haven't moved.

- **Fixed: the drawer's button labels smeared over the driver's row.** Part way through the drawer sliding open or shut, its button labels ("+1 Lap", "Warning", "Clear Penalties") were drawn over the driver's own row and stayed there. Each label now appears only once its button is fully inside the drawer.

- **Long lines like the recap stay in your ElevenLabs voice.** HeatCaller gave ElevenLabs 30 seconds for any one line, and a whole race recap on the eleven_v3 model can take longer than that on a perfectly good connection. The recap ran out of time, ElevenLabs was set aside as unreachable, Kokoro read the recap, and the booth interview and sign-off came back in the ElevenLabs voices - two voices in one program. A short race call still gets 30 seconds; a line written ahead of time gets a second more for every 25 characters past the first 300, up to two and a half minutes. Lines said live keep the 30 seconds, so a slow recap can't hold up the next race's calls.

- **The Technical Lead after heats too.** The pit-lane interview with the winner's Technical Lead at the flag is for mains. A new tick box on **Voice → Cast → Technical Lead**, *After heats too, not only mains*, has it after every race. Off by default.

- **Podium videos on your club's page, with a QR code on the Big Screen.** After a race, the top three's highlight videos are put on your club's page, and a **PODIUM VIDEOS** strip with a big QR code comes up down the right of the Big Screen. Drivers scan it and get a page for that race with 1st, 2nd and 3rd in order, each with a player and a **Download video** button (the file is named like *A-Main - Bill Hargrove.mp4*). A driver's own night page lists their podium videos too. The strip comes down as soon as the next race is staged.
  - **Only ever 1st, 2nd and 3rd** go online - the app sends nothing else, and the relay refuses anything else whatever sends it. Every other driver's video stays on the laptop.
  - Videos are kept on the relay for a week, at most 120 per club and 1 GB, oldest first. The links are random, so nobody can browse a club's videos.
  - **Sounds → Recording** has a new tick box: *Put the podium's videos on the club's page, with a QR code on the Big Screen to download them*. On by default; it needs the club's page switched on (**Club Website**). Redeploy your relay (`fly deploy --ha=false`) before using it.

- **A highlight video for every driver, with captions.** After each race HeatCaller now makes an upright video (1080x1920, up to 55 seconds) for each driver out of the race recording: a title card with the club, the race and the driver, then their race drawn in your Look - position, a pop when they pass somebody, battle banners, laps and lap times, the top of the order - under the announcer calling them, with what's said on screen as captions, and an end card with the result, the top five and your club's handle. The finish is always kept.
  - Set it on **Sounds → Recording**: **Highlight videos:** *Off*, *The winner*, *The podium* or *Every driver* (the default is every driver), and **Club handle:** for the end card. It needs *A clip for each driver* ticked.
  - Videos are saved beside the clips in the night's **Highlights** folder. **Share video** in a driver's drawer on Race Control opens the folder with their newest video selected.

- **Click a driver to correct them; double-click for their lap breakdown.** The correction buttons under the Race Control leaderboard are gone, so the board has room for more drivers. Click a driver and a drawer opens under their row with **+1 Lap**, **-1 Lap**, **+5s**, **+10s**, **Warning**, **Clear Penalties**, **Share video** and **Lap breakdown**. Click them again, or another driver, to close it. Double-click a driver to open their lap breakdown straight away, drawer or not.
  - **Clear Penalties** now clears warnings too.
  - The lap breakdown opens and closes on a reliable toggle, under the drawer.
  - **Start races On Air**, **Race Format...** and **Columns...** moved to the foot of the board.

- **On Air shows it's working.** Pressing On Air used to sit for up to 20 seconds while the opening was written and voiced, with nothing to show for it. The button now fills as it goes and counts down - *"12s · Writing"*, *"Voicing"*, *"Intros"*, *"Finishing"* - from how long the last few opens took. The opening and the driver introductions are also voiced ahead between races where they can be, so On Air is often ready almost at once.

- **Hold says it's on.** With **Hold** pressed, the button turns amber and reads **HELD · Release**, so a show that has stopped after the flag is obviously held rather than broken.

- **Flags pressed during a demo's On Air start the demo.** Pressing **Stage**, **Countdown** or **Green** while On Air was opening a demo called the demo off and started a real race with no cars. It now skips the rest of the opening and starts the demo, which stages and goes green by itself.

- **A Mission Control button on Race Control.** **Mission Control** now sits under **Pre-race check...**. The **Laps** and **Fastest** tiles beside the clock are gone.

- **Sponsor reads between races are now opt-in.** Paid reads are heard in the commercial break. To also put them on rotation in the quiet between races, tick **Also put reads on rotation between races, outside the commercial break** on the **Sponsors** page.

- **Pronouns for every driver, and the AI commentary uses them.** A driver's profile has a new **Pronouns:** box, right under **Announcer name:** - pick he/him, she/her or they/them, or type their own (like she/they). The AI commentary is handed a driver's pronouns beside their name whenever it writes about them - race recaps, segments, introductions, interviews, the night's pieces - and told to use them, instead of guessing from the name. The Team Principal's notes about a driver and the announcer's "right on his tail" in a running order follow them too. Leave it blank and nothing changes. What's typed is kept short and tidy, so it can only ever be read as pronouns.

- **Sponsors' taglines are said with their credits.** A sponsor with a **One-line tagline:** on the **Sponsors** page now has it read straight after every credit for a moment they present - *"That fastest lap is brought to you by Joe's Hobby Shop! Everything you need to go faster."* - on the calls during a race, at the top of their recap show segments, and with the driver introductions and the race recap. HeatCaller says the credit and the tagline itself, word for word, rather than asking the AI to: the AI is no longer told about the sponsor in a written battle or pace segment, the credit goes on the end of what it writes. Credits are still rationed as before, so a fastest-lap sponsor's tagline is heard at most once every three minutes. Paid reads are unchanged - the tagline is still in their first read of the night. A sponsor with no tagline sounds exactly as before.

- **The corrections drawer slides open.** Clicking a driver on the Race Control leaderboard opened their drawer of corrections (+1 Lap, +5s, Warning and the rest) in a single frame, and the rows below jumped down with it. It now slides open and shut, the buttons coming down out from under the driver's row, and moving it to another driver closes one as the other opens. A car crossing the line mid-slide doesn't restart it, and the buttons can be pressed as soon as it's fully open.

- **On Air stays one size, and turns cyan while you're live.** The **On Air** button changed width with every caption - "On Air", the countdown, "On Air · Skip" - so the flags beside it shuffled about during the show. Every button on the flag row - **On Air**, **Stage**, **Countdown**, **Green**, **Yellow**, **Red**, **Checkered** and **Reset** - is now the same width, wide enough for On Air's longest caption, and worked out again if you change the text size. If the countdown's "12s · Voicing" would not fit, it shows just "12s". From the jingle until the race is staged the button reads **On Air · Skip** (it said "Live · Skip") and is lit cyan instead of red, so a glance at the flag row tells you the room is on air.

- **On the recap show, the sponsor comes first.** A sponsor is now credited at the top of the segment they present, the way a TV broadcast does it - *"This race recap is brought to you by Joe's Hobby Shop!"* and then the recap - instead of at the end. This covers the race recap (whoever presents the race result) and the segments written into the show after it, such as milestones and the qualifying report. HeatCaller says the credit itself, so it is always first and the name is always said exactly as written; the AI is no longer told about the sponsor, so it can't put a credit at the end as well.
